192#include <MOM_memory.h>

194! A note on unit descriptions in comments: MOM6 uses units that can be rescaled for dimensional
195! consistency testing. These are noted in comments with units like Z, H, L, and T, along with
196! their mks counterparts with notation like "a velocity [Z T-1 ~> m s-1]". If the units
197! vary with the Boussinesq approximation, the Boussinesq variant is given first.
198
199!> A structure with diagnostic IDs of the state variables
200type MOM_diag_IDs
201 !>@{ 3-d state field diagnostic IDs
202 integer :: id_u = -1, id_v = -1, id_h = -1
203 !>@}
204 !> 2-d state field diagnostic ID
205 integer :: id_ssh_inst = -1
206end type MOM_diag_IDs
207
208!> Control structure for the MOM module, including the variables that describe
209!! the state of the ocean.
210type, public :: MOM_control_struct ; private
211 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_,NKMEM_) :: &
212 h, & !< layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
213 T, & !< potential temperature [C ~> degC]
214 S !< salinity [S ~> ppt]
215 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEM_,NKMEM_) :: &
216 u, & !< zonal velocity component [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
217 uh, & !< uh = u * h * dy at u grid points [H L2 T-1 ~> m3 s-1 or kg s-1]
218 uhtr !< accumulated zonal thickness fluxes to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
219 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEMB_PTR_,NKMEM_) :: &
220 v, & !< meridional velocity [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
221 vh, & !< vh = v * h * dx at v grid points [H L2 T-1 ~> m3 s-1 or kg s-1]
222 vhtr !< accumulated meridional thickness fluxes to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
223 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: ssh_rint
224 !< A running time integral of the sea surface height [T Z ~> s m].
225 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: ave_ssh_ibc
226 !< time-averaged (over a forcing time step) sea surface height
227 !! with a correction for the inverse barometer [Z ~> m]
228 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: eta_av_bc
229 !< free surface height or column mass time averaged over the last
230 !! baroclinic dynamics time step [H ~> m or kg m-2]
231 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: Hml => NULL()
232 !< active mixed layer depth, or 0 if there is no boundary layer scheme [Z ~> m]
233 real :: time_in_cycle !< The running time of the current time-stepping cycle
234 !! in calls that step the dynamics, and also the length of
235 !! the time integral of ssh_rint [T ~> s].
236 real :: time_in_thermo_cycle !< The running time of the current time-stepping
237 !! cycle in calls that step the thermodynamics [T ~> s].
238
239 type(ocean_grid_type), allocatable :: G_in !< Input grid metric
240 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G => NULL() !< Model grid metric
241 logical :: rotate_index = .false. !< True if index map is rotated
242 logical :: homogenize_forcings = .false. !< True if all inputs are homogenized
243 logical :: update_ustar = .false. !< True to update ustar from homogenized tau
244 logical :: vertex_shear = .false. !< True if vertex shear is on
245
246 type(verticalGrid_type), pointer :: &
247 GV => NULL() !< structure containing vertical grid info
248 type(unit_scale_type), pointer :: &
249 US => NULL() !< structure containing various unit conversion factors
250 type(thermo_var_ptrs), allocatable :: tv
251 !< structure containing pointers to available thermodynamic fields
252 real :: t_dyn_rel_adv !< The time of the dynamics relative to tracer advection and lateral mixing
253 !! [T ~> s], or equivalently the elapsed time since advectively updating the
254 !! tracers. t_dyn_rel_adv is invariably positive and may span multiple coupling timesteps.
255 integer :: n_dyn_steps_in_adv !< The number of dynamics time steps that contributed to uhtr
256 !! and vhtr since the last time tracer advection occured.
257 real :: t_dyn_rel_thermo !< The time of the dynamics relative to diabatic processes and remapping
258 !! [T ~> s]. t_dyn_rel_thermo can be negative or positive depending on whether
259 !! the diabatic processes are applied before or after the dynamics and may span
260 !! multiple coupling timesteps.
261 real :: t_dyn_rel_diag !< The time of the diagnostics relative to diabatic processes and remapping
262 !! [T ~> s]. t_dyn_rel_diag is always positive, since the diagnostics must lag.
263 logical :: preadv_h_stored = .false. !< If true, the thicknesses from before the advective cycle
264 !! have been stored for use in diagnostics.
265
266 type(diag_ctrl) :: diag !< structure to regulate diagnostic output timing
267 type(vertvisc_type), allocatable :: visc
268 !< structure containing vertical viscosities, bottom drag viscosities, and related fields
269 type(MEKE_type) :: MEKE !< Fields related to the Mesoscale Eddy Kinetic Energy
270 logical :: adiabatic !< If true, there are no diapycnal mass fluxes, and no calls
271 !! to routines to calculate or apply diapycnal fluxes.
272 logical :: diabatic_first !< If true, apply diabatic and thermodynamic processes before time
273 !! stepping the dynamics.
274 logical :: use_ALE_algorithm !< If true, use the ALE algorithm rather than layered
275 !! isopycnal/stacked shallow water mode. This logical is set by calling the
276 !! function useRegridding() from the MOM_regridding module.
277 logical :: remap_aux_vars !< If true, apply ALE remapping to all of the auxiliary 3-D
278 !! variables that are needed to reproduce across restarts,
279 !! similarly to what is done with the primary state variables.
280 logical :: remap_uv_using_old_alg !< If true, use the old "remapping via a delta z" method for
281 !! velocities. If false, remap between two grids described by thicknesses.
282
283 type(MOM_stoch_eos_CS) :: stoch_eos_CS !< structure containing random pattern for stoch EOS
284 logical :: alternate_first_direction !< If true, alternate whether the x- or y-direction
285 !! updates occur first in directionally split parts of the calculation.
286 real :: first_dir_restart = -1.0 !< A real copy of G%first_direction for use in restart files [nondim]
287 logical :: offline_tracer_mode = .false.
288 !< If true, step_offline() is called instead of step_MOM().
289 !! This is intended for running MOM6 in offline tracer mode
290 logical :: MEKE_in_dynamics !< If .true. (default), MEKE is called in the dynamics routine otherwise
291 !! it is called during the tracer dynamics
292
293 type(time_type), pointer :: Time !< pointer to the ocean clock
294 real :: dt !< (baroclinic) dynamics time step [T ~> s]
295 real :: dt_therm !< diabatic time step [T ~> s]
296 real :: dt_tr_adv !< tracer advection time step [T ~> s]
297 logical :: thermo_spans_coupling !< If true, thermodynamic and tracer time
298 !! steps can span multiple coupled time steps.
299 logical :: tradv_spans_coupling !< If true, thermodynamic and tracer time
300 integer :: nstep_tot = 0 !< The total number of dynamic timesteps taken
301 !! so far in this run segment
302 logical :: count_calls = .false. !< If true, count the calls to step_MOM, rather than the
303 !! number of dynamics steps in nstep_tot
304 logical :: debug !< If true, write verbose checksums for debugging purposes.
305 logical :: debug_OBCs !< If true, write verbose OBC values for debugging purposes.
306 integer :: ntrunc !< number u,v truncations since last call to write_energy
307
308 integer :: cont_stencil !< The stencil for thickness from the continuity solver.
309 integer :: dyn_h_stencil !< The stencil for thickness for the dynamics based on
310 !! the continuity solver and Coriolis schemes.
311 ! These elements are used to control the dynamics updates.
312 logical :: do_dynamics !< If false, does not call step_MOM_dyn_*. This is an
313 !! undocumented run-time flag that is fragile.
314 logical :: split !< If true, use the split time stepping scheme.
315 logical :: use_alt_split !< If true, use a version of the split explicit time stepping
316 !! scheme that exchanges velocities with step_MOM that have the
317 !! average barotropic phase over a baroclinic timestep rather
318 !! than the instantaneous barotropic phase.
319 logical :: use_RK2 !< If true, use RK2 instead of RK3 in unsplit mode
320 !! (i.e., no split between barotropic and baroclinic).
321 logical :: interface_filter !< If true, apply an interface height filter immediately
322 !! after any calls to thickness_diffuse.
323 logical :: thickness_diffuse !< If true, diffuse interface height w/ a diffusivity KHTH.
324 logical :: thickness_diffuse_first !< If true, diffuse thickness before dynamics.
325 logical :: interface_filter_dt_bug !< If true, uses the wrong time interval in
326 !! calls to interface_filter and thickness_diffuse.
327 logical :: mixedlayer_restrat !< If true, use submesoscale mixed layer restratifying scheme.
328 logical :: useMEKE !< If true, call the MEKE parameterization.
329 logical :: use_stochastic_EOS !< If true, use the stochastic EOS parameterizations.
330 logical :: useWaves !< If true, update Stokes drift
331 logical :: StokesMOST !< If true, use Stokes Similarity package. Needed to decide if Lam2 should
332 !! be passed to mixedlayer_restrat.
333 logical :: wave_enhanced_ustar !< If true, enhance ustar in Bodner23. Needed to decide if Lam2 should
334 !! be passed to mixedlayer_restrat.
335 real :: dtbt_reset_period !< The time interval between dynamic recalculation of the
336 !! barotropic time step [T ~> s]. If this is negative dtbt is never
337 !! calculated, and if it is 0, dtbt is calculated every step.
338 type(time_type) :: dtbt_reset_interval !< A time_time representation of dtbt_reset_period.
339 type(time_type) :: dtbt_reset_time !< The next time DTBT should be calculated.
340 real :: dt_obc_seg_period !< The time interval between OBC segment updates for OBGC
341 !! tracers [T ~> s], or a negative value if the segment
342 !! data are time-invarant, or zero to update the OBGC
343 !! segment data with every call to update_OBC_tracer_data.
344 type(time_type) :: dt_obc_seg_interval !< A time_time representation of dt_obc_seg_period.
345 type(time_type) :: dt_obc_seg_time !< The next time OBC segment update is applied to OBGC tracers.
346
347 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: frac_shelf_h => NULL() !< fraction of total area occupied
348 !! by ice shelf [nondim]
349 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: mass_shelf => NULL() !< Mass of ice shelf [R Z ~> kg m-2]
350 type(accel_diag_ptrs), allocatable :: ADp !< structure containing pointers to accelerations,
351 !! for derived diagnostics (e.g., energy budgets)
352 type(cont_diag_ptrs) :: CDp !< structure containing pointers to continuity equation
353 !! terms, for derived diagnostics (e.g., energy budgets)
354 real, dimension(:,:,:), pointer :: &
355 u_prev => NULL(), & !< previous value of u stored for diagnostics [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
356 v_prev => NULL() !< previous value of v stored for diagnostics [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
357
358 logical :: interp_p_surf !< If true, linearly interpolate surface pressure
359 !! over the coupling time step, using specified value
360 !! at the end of the coupling step. False by default.
361 logical :: p_surf_prev_set !< If true, p_surf_prev has been properly set from
362 !! a previous time-step or the ocean restart file.
363 !! This is only valid when interp_p_surf is true.
364 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: &
365 p_surf_prev => NULL(), & !< surface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] at end previous call to step_MOM
366 p_surf_begin => NULL(), & !< surface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] at start of step_MOM_dyn_...
367 p_surf_end => NULL() !< surface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] at end of step_MOM_dyn_...
368
369 ! Variables needed to reach between start and finish phases of initialization
370 logical :: write_IC !< If true, then the initial conditions will be written to file
371 character(len=120) :: IC_file !< A file into which the initial conditions are
372 !! written in a new run if SAVE_INITIAL_CONDS is true.
373
374 logical :: calc_rho_for_sea_lev !< If true, calculate rho to convert pressure to sea level
375
376 ! These elements are used to control the calculation and error checking of the surface state
377 real :: Hmix !< Diagnostic mixed layer thickness over which to
378 !! average surface tracer properties when a bulk
379 !! mixed layer is not used [H ~> m or kg m-2], or a negative value
380 !! if a bulk mixed layer is being used.
381 real :: HFrz !< If HFrz > 0, the nominal depth over which melt potential is computed
382 !! [H ~> m or kg m-2]. The actual depth over which melt potential is
383 !! computed is min(HFrz, OBLD), where OBLD is the boundary layer depth.
384 !! If HFrz <= 0 (default), melt potential will not be computed.
385 real :: Hmix_UV !< Depth scale over which to average surface flow to
386 !! feedback to the coupler/driver [H ~> m or kg m-2] when
387 !! bulk mixed layer is not used, or a negative value
388 !! if a bulk mixed layer is being used.
389 logical :: check_bad_sfc_vals !< If true, scan surface state for ridiculous values.
390 real :: bad_val_ssh_max !< Maximum SSH before triggering bad value message [Z ~> m]
391 real :: bad_val_sst_max !< Maximum SST before triggering bad value message [C ~> degC]
392 real :: bad_val_sst_min !< Minimum SST before triggering bad value message [C ~> degC]
393 real :: bad_val_sss_max !< Maximum SSS before triggering bad value message [S ~> ppt]
394 real :: bad_val_col_thick !< Minimum column thickness before triggering bad value message [Z ~> m]
395 integer :: answer_date !< The vintage of the expressions for the surface properties. Values
396 !! below 20190101 recover the answers from the end of 2018, while
397 !! higher values use more appropriate expressions that differ at
398 !! roundoff for non-Boussinesq cases.
399 logical :: use_particles !< Turns on the particles package
400 logical :: use_uh_particles !< particles are advected by uh/h
401 logical :: uh_particles_bug !< If true, uses an inconsistent timestep for particle advection
402 logical :: use_dbclient !< Turns on the database client used for ML inference/analysis
403 character(len=10) :: particle_type !< Particle types include: surface(default), profiling and sail drone.
404
405 type(MOM_diag_IDs) :: IDs !< Handles used for diagnostics.
406 type(transport_diag_IDs) :: transport_IDs !< Handles used for transport diagnostics.
407 type(surface_diag_IDs) :: sfc_IDs !< Handles used for surface diagnostics.
408 type(diag_grid_storage) :: diag_pre_sync !< The grid (thicknesses) before remapping
409 type(diag_grid_storage) :: diag_pre_dyn !< The grid (thicknesses) before dynamics
410
411 logical :: accumulate_resolved_flux = .false. !< If true, accumulate resolved flux for tracers for diagnostics
412 !! separating the tracer flux due to resolved and parameterized flow
413 logical :: do_resolved_advection = .false. !< If true, calculate advection by resolved flow
414 logical :: do_param_advection = .false. !< If true, calculate advection by parameterized flow
415 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: &
416 uhtr_resolved !< accumulated zonal thickness fluxes due to resolved flow to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
417 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: &
418 vhtr_resolved !< accumulated meridional thickness fluxes due to resolved flow to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
419

421
422 type(MOM_dyn_unsplit_CS), pointer :: dyn_unsplit_CSp => NULL()
423 !< Pointer to the control structure used for the unsplit dynamics

428 type(MOM_dyn_split_RK2b_CS), pointer :: dyn_split_RK2b_CSp => NULL()

430 type(harmonic_analysis_CS), pointer :: HA_CSp => NULL()
431 !< Pointer to the control structure for harmonic analysis
432 type(thickness_diffuse_CS) :: thickness_diffuse_CSp

434 !! This is also common referred to as Gent-McWilliams diffusion
435 type(interface_filter_CS) :: interface_filter_CSp

437 type(mixedlayer_restrat_CS) :: mixedlayer_restrat_CSp

445 type(VarMix_CS), allocatable :: VarMix
446 !< Control structure for the variable mixing module
447 type(tracer_registry_type), pointer :: tracer_Reg => NULL()
448 !< Pointer to the MOM tracer registry
449 type(tracer_advect_CS), pointer :: tracer_adv_CSp => NULL()

451 type(tracer_hor_diff_CS), pointer :: tracer_diff_CSp => NULL()
452 !< Pointer to the MOM along-isopycnal tracer diffusion control structure
453 type(tracer_flow_control_CS), pointer :: tracer_flow_CSp => NULL()
454 !< Pointer to the control structure that orchestrates the calling of tracer packages
455 ! Although update_OBC_CS is not used directly outside of initialization, other modules
456 ! set pointers to this type, so it should be kept for the duration of the run.
457 type(update_OBC_CS), pointer :: update_OBC_CSp => NULL()
458 !< Pointer to the control structure for updating open boundary condition properties
459 type(ocean_OBC_type), pointer :: OBC => NULL()
460 !< Pointer to the MOM open boundary condition type
461 type(sponge_CS), pointer :: sponge_CSp => NULL()
462 !< Pointer to the layered-mode sponge control structure
463 type(ALE_sponge_CS), pointer :: ALE_sponge_CSp => NULL()
464 !< Pointer to the ALE-mode sponge control structure
465 type(oda_incupd_CS), pointer :: oda_incupd_CSp => NULL()
466 !< Pointer to the oda incremental update control structure
467 type(int_tide_CS), pointer :: int_tide_CSp => NULL()
468 !< Pointer to the internal tides control structure
469 type(ALE_CS), pointer :: ALE_CSp => NULL()
470 !< Pointer to the Arbitrary Lagrangian Eulerian (ALE) vertical coordinate control structure
471
472 ! Pointers to control structures used for diagnostics
473 type(sum_output_CS), pointer :: sum_output_CSp => NULL()
474 !< Pointer to the globally summed output control structure
475 type(diagnostics_CS) :: diagnostics_CSp
476 !< Pointer to the MOM diagnostics control structure
477 type(offline_transport_CS), pointer :: offline_CSp => NULL()
478 !< Pointer to the offline tracer transport control structure
479 type(porous_barrier_CS) :: por_bar_CS
480 !< Control structure for porous barrier
481
482 logical :: ensemble_ocean !< if true, this run is part of a
483 !! larger ensemble for the purpose of data assimilation
484 !! or statistical analysis.
485 type(ODA_CS), pointer :: odaCS => NULL() !< a pointer to the control structure for handling
486 !! ensemble model state vectors and data assimilation
487 !! increments and priors
488 type(dbcomms_CS_type) :: dbcomms_CS !< Control structure for database client used for online ML/AI
489 logical :: use_porbar !< If true, use porous barrier to constrain the widths and face areas
490 !! at the edges of the grid cells.
491 type(porous_barrier_type) :: pbv !< porous barrier fractional cell metrics
492 type(particles), pointer :: particles => NULL() !<Lagrangian particles
493 type(stochastic_CS), pointer :: stoch_CS => NULL() !< a pointer to the stochastics control structure
494 type(MOM_restart_CS), pointer :: restart_CS => NULL()
495 !< Pointer to MOM's restart control structure
496end type MOM_control_struct
497
498public initialize_MOM, finish_MOM_initialization, MOM_end
499public step_MOM, step_offline
500public extract_surface_state, get_ocean_stocks
501public get_MOM_state_elements, MOM_state_is_synchronized
502public allocate_surface_state, deallocate_surface_state
503public save_MOM_restart

505!>@{ CPU time clock IDs
506integer :: id_clock_ocean
507integer :: id_clock_dynamics
508integer :: id_clock_thermo
509integer :: id_clock_MOM_end
510integer :: id_clock_remap
511integer :: id_clock_tracer
512integer :: id_clock_diabatic
513integer :: id_clock_adiabatic
514integer :: id_clock_continuity ! also in dynamics s/r
515integer :: id_clock_thick_diff
516integer :: id_clock_int_filter
517integer :: id_clock_BBL_visc
518integer :: id_clock_ml_restrat
519integer :: id_clock_diagnostics
520integer :: id_clock_Z_diag
521integer :: id_clock_init
522integer :: id_clock_MOM_init
523integer :: id_clock_pass ! also in dynamics d/r
524integer :: id_clock_pass_init ! also in dynamics d/r
525integer :: id_clock_ALE
526integer :: id_clock_other
527integer :: id_clock_offline_tracer

529integer :: id_clock_unit_tests
530integer :: id_clock_stoch
531integer :: id_clock_varT

535
536!> This subroutine orchestrates the time stepping of MOM. The adiabatic
537!! dynamics are stepped by calls to one of the step_MOM_dyn_...routines.
538!! The action of lateral processes on tracers occur in calls to
539!! advect_tracer and tracer_hordiff. Vertical mixing and possibly remapping
540!! occur inside of diabatic.
541360subroutine step_MOM(forces_in, fluxes_in, sfc_state, Time_start, time_int_in, CS, &
542 Waves, do_dynamics, do_thermodynamics, start_cycle, &
543 end_cycle, cycle_length, reset_therm)
544 type(mech_forcing), target, intent(inout) :: forces_in !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
545 type(forcing), target, intent(inout) :: fluxes_in !< A structure with pointers to themodynamic,
546 !! tracer and mass exchange forcing fields
547 type(surface), target, intent(inout) :: sfc_state !< surface ocean state
548 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time_start !< starting time of a segment, as a time type
549 real, intent(in) :: time_int_in !< time interval covered by this run segment [T ~> s].
550 type(MOM_control_struct), intent(inout), target :: CS !< control structure from initialize_MOM
551 type(Wave_parameters_CS), &
552 optional, pointer :: Waves !< An optional pointer to a wave property CS
553 logical, optional, intent(in) :: do_dynamics !< Present and false, do not do updates due
554 !! to the dynamics.
555 logical, optional, intent(in) :: do_thermodynamics !< Present and false, do not do updates due
556 !! to the thermodynamics or remapping.
557 logical, optional, intent(in) :: start_cycle !< This indicates whether this call is to be
558 !! treated as the first call to step_MOM in a
559 !! time-stepping cycle; missing is like true.
560 logical, optional, intent(in) :: end_cycle !< This indicates whether this call is to be
561 !! treated as the last call to step_MOM in a
562 !! time-stepping cycle; missing is like true.
563 real, optional, intent(in) :: cycle_length !< The amount of time in a coupled time
564 !! stepping cycle [T ~> s].
565 logical, optional, intent(in) :: reset_therm !< This indicates whether the running sums of
566 !! thermodynamic quantities should be reset.
567 !! If missing, this is like start_cycle.
568
569 ! local variables
570 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G => NULL() ! pointer to a structure containing
571 ! metrics and related information
572 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G_in => NULL() ! Input grid metric
573 type(verticalGrid_type), pointer :: GV => NULL() ! Pointer to the vertical grid structure
574 type(unit_scale_type), pointer :: US => NULL() ! Pointer to a structure containing
575 ! various unit conversion factors
576 integer :: ntstep ! number of time steps between diabatic forcing updates
577 integer :: ntastep ! number of time steps between tracer advection updates
578 integer :: n_max ! number of steps to take in this call
579 integer :: halo_sz, dynamics_stencil
580
581 integer :: i, j, k, is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, nz, n
582 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
583
584 real :: time_interval ! time interval covered by this run segment [T ~> s].
585 real :: dt ! baroclinic time step [T ~> s]
586 real :: dtdia ! time step for diabatic processes [T ~> s]
587 real :: dt_tr_adv ! time step for tracer advection [T ~> s]
588 real :: dt_therm ! a limited and quantized version of CS%dt_therm [T ~> s]
589 real :: dt_tradv_here ! a further limited value of dt_tr_adv [T ~> s]
590
591 real :: wt_end, wt_beg ! Fractional weights of the future pressure at the end
592 ! and beginning of the current time step [nondim]
593 real :: bbl_time_int ! The amount of time over which the calculated BBL
594 ! properties will apply, for use in diagnostics, or 0
595 ! if it is not to be calculated anew [T ~> s].
596 real :: rel_time = 0.0 ! relative time since start of this call [T ~> s].
597
598 logical :: do_advection ! If true, do tracer advection.
599 logical :: do_diabatic ! If true, do diabatic update.
600 logical :: thermo_does_span_coupling ! If true,thermodynamic (diabatic) forcing spans
601 ! multiple coupling timesteps.
602 logical :: tradv_does_span_coupling ! If true, tracer advection spans
603 ! multiple coupling timesteps.
604 logical :: do_dyn ! If true, dynamics are updated with this call.
605 logical :: do_thermo ! If true, thermodynamics and remapping may be applied with this call.
606 logical :: debug_redundant ! If true, check redundant values on PE boundaries when debugging.
607 logical :: nonblocking_p_surf_update ! A flag to indicate whether surface properties
608 ! can use nonblocking halo updates
609 logical :: cycle_start ! If true, do calculations that are only done at the start of
610 ! a stepping cycle (whatever that may mean).
611 logical :: cycle_end ! If true, do calculations and diagnostics that are only done at
612 ! the end of a stepping cycle (whatever that may mean).
613 logical :: therm_reset ! If true, reset running sums of thermodynamic quantities.
614 real :: cycle_time ! The length of the coupled time-stepping cycle [T ~> s].
615 real, dimension(SZI_(CS%G),SZJ_(CS%G)) :: &
616720 U_star ! The wind friction velocity, calculated using the Boussinesq reference density or
617 ! the time-evolving surface density in non-Boussinesq mode [Z T-1 ~> m s-1]
618 real, dimension(SZI_(CS%G),SZJ_(CS%G)) :: &
619720 ssh ! sea surface height, which may be based on eta_av [Z ~> m]
620 real, dimension(SZI_(CS%G),SZJ_(CS%G),SZK_(CS%GV)) :: &
621720 dz ! Vertical distance across layers [Z ~> m]
622
623 real, dimension(:,:,:), pointer :: &
624 u => NULL(), & ! u : zonal velocity component [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
625 v => NULL(), & ! v : meridional velocity component [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
626 h => NULL() ! h : layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
627 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: &
628 p_surf => NULL() ! A pointer to the ocean surface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa].
629 real :: I_wt_ssh ! The inverse of the time weights [T-1 ~> s-1]
630
631 type(time_type) :: Time_local, end_time_thermo
632 type(time_type) :: Time_end_diag ! End time of a diagnostic segment, as a time type
633
634 type(group_pass_type) :: pass_tau_ustar_psurf
635 logical :: showCallTree
636
637 ! External forcing fields on the model index map
638 type(mech_forcing), pointer :: forces ! Mechanical forcing
639 type(forcing), pointer :: fluxes ! Boundary fluxes
640 type(surface), pointer :: sfc_state_diag ! Surface boundary fields
641 integer :: turns ! Number of quarter turns from input to model indexing
642
643360 G => CS%G ; G_in => CS%G_in ; GV => CS%GV ; US => CS%US
644360 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke
645360 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
646360 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
647360 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
648360 u => CS%u ; v => CS%v ; h => CS%h
649
650360 time_interval = time_int_in
6510 do_dyn = .true. ; if (present(do_dynamics)) do_dyn = do_dynamics
652360 do_thermo = .true. ; if (present(do_thermodynamics)) do_thermo = do_thermodynamics
653360 if (.not.(do_dyn .or. do_thermo)) call MOM_error(FATAL,"Step_MOM: "//&
6540 "Both do_dynamics and do_thermodynamics are false, which makes no sense.")
655360 cycle_start = .true. ; if (present(start_cycle)) cycle_start = start_cycle
656360 cycle_end = .true. ; if (present(end_cycle)) cycle_end = end_cycle
657360 cycle_time = time_interval ; if (present(cycle_length)) cycle_time = cycle_length
658360 therm_reset = cycle_start ; if (present(reset_therm)) therm_reset = reset_therm
659
660360 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_ocean)
661360 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_other)
662
663360 if (CS%debug) then
664 !$omp target update from(u, v, h)
6650 call query_debugging_checks(do_redundant=debug_redundant)
6660 call MOM_state_chksum("Beginning of step_MOM ", u, v, h, CS%uh, CS%vh, G, GV, US)
667 endif
668
669360 showCallTree = callTree_showQuery()
670360 if (showCallTree) call callTree_enter("step_MOM(), MOM.F90")
671
672 ! Rotate the forces from G_in to G
673360 if (CS%rotate_index) then
6740 turns = G%HI%turns
6750 allocate(forces)
6760 call allocate_mech_forcing(forces_in, G, forces)
6770 call rotate_mech_forcing(forces_in, turns, forces)
678
6790 allocate(fluxes)
6800 call allocate_forcing_type(fluxes_in, G, fluxes, turns=turns)
6810 call rotate_forcing(fluxes_in, fluxes, turns)
682 else
683360 forces => forces_in
684360 fluxes => fluxes_in
685 endif
686
687 ! Homogenize the forces
688360 if (CS%homogenize_forcings) then
689 ! Homogenize all forcing and fluxes fields.
6900 call homogenize_mech_forcing(forces, G, US, GV%Rho0, CS%update_ustar)
691 ! Note the following computes the mean ustar as the mean of ustar rather than
692 ! ustar of the mean of tau.
6930 call homogenize_forcing(fluxes, G, GV, US)
6940 if (CS%update_ustar) then
695 ! These calls corrects the ustar values
6960 call copy_common_forcing_fields(forces, fluxes, G)
6970 call set_derived_forcing_fields(forces, fluxes, G, US, GV%Rho0)
698 endif
699 endif
700 !$omp target enter data map(to: forces, forces%taux, forces%tauy, forces%ustar)
701
702 ! This will be replaced later with the pressures from forces or fluxes if they are available.
703360 if (associated(CS%tv%p_surf)) CS%tv%p_surf(:,:) = 0.0
704
705 ! First determine the time step that is consistent with this call and an
706 ! integer fraction of time_interval.
707360 if (do_dyn) then
708360 n_max = 1
709360 if (time_interval > CS%dt) n_max = ceiling(time_interval/CS%dt - 0.001)
710
711360 dt = time_interval / real(n_max)
712 thermo_does_span_coupling = (CS%thermo_spans_coupling .and. &
713360 (CS%dt_therm > 1.5*cycle_time))
714 tradv_does_span_coupling = (CS%tradv_spans_coupling .and. &
715360 (CS%dt_tr_adv > 1.5*cycle_time))
716360 if (thermo_does_span_coupling) then
717 ! Set dt_therm to be an integer multiple of the coupling time step.
7180 dt_therm = cycle_time * floor(CS%dt_therm / cycle_time + 0.001)
7190 ntstep = floor(dt_therm/dt + 0.001)
720360 elseif (.not.do_thermo) then
7210 dt_therm = CS%dt_therm
7220 if (present(cycle_length)) dt_therm = min(CS%dt_therm, cycle_length)
7230 ntstep = 1 ! ntstep is initialized to avoid an error in a secondary logical test,
724 ! but the nonzero value of ntstep does not matter when do_thermo is false.
725 else
726360 ntstep = MAX(1, MIN(n_max, floor(CS%dt_therm/dt + 0.001)))
727360 dt_therm = dt*ntstep
728 endif
729360 if (tradv_does_span_coupling) then
730 ! Set dt_tr_adv to be an integer multiple of the coupling time step.
7310 dt_tr_adv = cycle_time * floor(CS%dt_tr_adv / cycle_time + 0.001)
7320 ntastep = floor(dt_tr_adv/dt + 0.001)
733360 elseif (.not.do_thermo) then
7340 dt_tr_adv = CS%dt_tr_adv
7350 if (present(cycle_length)) dt_tr_adv = min(CS%dt_tr_adv, cycle_length)
736 ! ntastep is not used.
737 else
738360 ntastep = MAX(1, MIN(n_max, floor(CS%dt_tr_adv/dt + 0.001)))
739360 dt_tr_adv = dt*ntastep
740 endif
741
742 !---------- Initiate group halo pass of the forcing fields
743360 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_pass)
744 ! Halo updates for surface pressure need to be completed before calling calc_resoln_function
745 ! among other routines if the surface pressure is used in the equation of state.
746 nonblocking_p_surf_update = G%nonblocking_updates .and. &
747 .not.(associated(CS%tv%p_surf) .and. associated(forces%p_surf) .and. &
748360 allocated(CS%tv%SpV_avg) .and. associated(CS%tv%T))
749360 if (.not.associated(forces%taux) .or. .not.associated(forces%tauy)) &
7500 call MOM_error(FATAL,'step_MOM:forces%taux,tauy not associated')
751360 call create_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, forces%taux, forces%tauy, G%Domain)
752360 if (associated(forces%ustar)) &
753360 call create_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, forces%ustar, G%Domain)
754360 if (associated(forces%tau_mag)) &
755360 call create_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, forces%tau_mag, G%Domain)
756360 if (associated(forces%p_surf)) &
757360 call create_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, forces%p_surf, G%Domain)
758360 if (nonblocking_p_surf_update) then
7590 call start_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, G%Domain)
760 else
761360 call do_group_pass(pass_tau_ustar_psurf, G%Domain, omp_offload=.true.)
762 endif
763360 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_pass)
764
765360 if (associated(forces%p_surf)) p_surf => forces%p_surf
766360 if (.not.associated(forces%p_surf)) CS%interp_p_surf = .false.
767360 if (associated(CS%tv%p_surf) .and. associated(forces%p_surf)) then
7680 do j=jsd,jed ; do i=isd,ied ; CS%tv%p_surf(i,j) = forces%p_surf(i,j) ; enddo ; enddo
769
7700 if (allocated(CS%tv%SpV_avg) .and. associated(CS%tv%T)) then
771 !$omp target update from(h)
772 ! The internal ocean state depends on the surface pressues, so update SpV_avg.
7730 dynamics_stencil = min(3, G%Domain%nihalo, G%Domain%njhalo)
7740 call calc_derived_thermo(CS%tv, h, G, GV, US, halo=dynamics_stencil, debug=CS%debug)
775 endif
776 endif
777
778 else
779 ! This step only updates the thermodynamics so setting timesteps is simpler.
7800 n_max = 1
7810 if ((time_interval > CS%dt_therm) .and. (CS%dt_therm > 0.0)) &
7820 n_max = ceiling(time_interval/CS%dt_therm - 0.001)
783

8000 halo_sz = max(halo_sz, 1)

802 endif

806360 if (therm_reset) then

817360 if (cycle_start) then

839360 if (CS%interp_p_surf) then

8881080 do n=1,n_max
889720 rel_time = rel_time + dt ! The relative time at the end of the step.

891720 CS%Time = Time_start + real_to_time(rel_time - 0.5*dt, unscale=US%T_to_s)

902 ! This is the first place where the diabatic processes and remapping could occur.
903720 if (CS%diabatic_first .and. (CS%t_dyn_rel_adv==0.0) .and. do_thermo) then ! do thermodynamics.
9040 if (.not.do_dyn) then
9050 dtdia = dt
9060 elseif (thermo_does_span_coupling) then

9140 "with THERMO_SPANS_COUPLING and DIABATIC_FIRST.")

942

9450 if (showCallTree) call callTree_waypoint("finished diabatic_first (step_MOM)")
946
9470 if (dtdia > dt) & ! Reset CS%Time to its previous value.
9480 CS%Time = Time_start + real_to_time(rel_time - 0.5*dt, unscale=US%T_to_s)
949 endif ! end of block "(CS%diabatic_first .and. (CS%t_dyn_rel_adv==0.0))"
950
951720 if (do_dyn) then
952 ! Store pre-dynamics thicknesses for proper diagnostic remapping for transports or

955720 if (.not.CS%preadv_h_stored .and. (CS%t_dyn_rel_adv == 0.)) then

979720 dt_tradv_here = dt*min(ntstep, n_max-n+1)

984720 bbl_time_int = 0.0
985720 if (do_thermo) then
986720 if ((CS%t_dyn_rel_adv == 0.0) .or. (n==1)) &
987360 bbl_time_int = max(dt, min(dt_therm - CS%t_dyn_rel_adv, dt*(1+n_max-n)) )
988 else

993720 if (CS%interp_p_surf) then

10210 "with DIABATIC_FIRST.")
1022 endif

1072 else ! The diabatic processes and the dynamics are synchronized.

1121360 call diag_copy_diag_to_storage(CS%diag_pre_sync, h, CS%diag)

1129720 if (do_dyn .and. .not.CS%count_calls) CS%nstep_tot = CS%nstep_tot + 1

12180 call deallocate_surface_state(sfc_state_diag)
1219 endif
1220360 if (showCallTree) call callTree_waypoint("Done with end cycle diagnostics (step_MOM)")
1221 endif
1222
1223 ! Accumulate the surface fluxes for assessing conservation
1224360 if (do_thermo .and. fluxes%fluxes_used) then
1225 !$omp target update from(sfc_state%SST) &
1226 !$omp if(.not.associated(fluxes%heat_content_evap) .and. &
1227 !$omp .not.(associated(CS%tv%TempxPme) .and. &
1228 !$omp associated(fluxes%evap)))
1229 call accumulate_net_input(fluxes, sfc_state, CS%tv, fluxes%dt_buoy_accum, &
1230360 G, US, CS%sum_output_CSp)
1231 endif
1232
1233360 if (MOM_state_is_synchronized(CS)) then
1234 call write_energy(CS%u, CS%v, CS%h, CS%tv, Time_local, CS%nstep_tot, &
1235 G, GV, US, CS%sum_output_CSp, CS%tracer_flow_CSp, &
1236360 dt_forcing=real_to_time(time_interval, unscale=US%T_to_s) )
1237 endif
1238360 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_other)
1239
1240 ! De-rotate fluxes and copy back to the input, since they can be changed.
1241360 if (CS%rotate_index) then
12420 call rotate_forcing(fluxes, fluxes_in, -turns)
12430 call rotate_mech_forcing(forces, -turns, forces_in)
12440 call deallocate_mech_forcing(forces)
12450 deallocate(forces)
12460 call deallocate_forcing_type(fluxes)
12470 deallocate(fluxes)
1248 endif
1249
1250360 if (showCallTree) call callTree_leave("step_MOM()")
1251360 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_ocean)
1252
1253360end subroutine step_MOM
1254
1255!> Time step the ocean dynamics, including the momentum and continuity equations
1256720subroutine step_MOM_dynamics(forces, p_surf_begin, p_surf_end, dt, dt_tr_adv, &
1257 bbl_time_int, CS, Time_local, Waves)
1258 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
1259 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: p_surf_begin !< A pointer (perhaps NULL) to the surface
1260 !! pressure at the beginning of this dynamic
1261 !! step, intent in [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa].
1262 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: p_surf_end !< A pointer (perhaps NULL) to the surface
1263 !! pressure at the end of this dynamic step,
1264 !! intent in [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa].
1265 real, intent(in) :: dt !< time interval covered by this call [T ~> s].
1266 real, intent(in) :: dt_tr_adv !< time interval covered by any updates that may
1267 !! span multiple dynamics steps [T ~> s].
1268 real, intent(in) :: bbl_time_int !< time interval over which updates to the
1269 !! bottom boundary layer properties will apply [T ~> s],
1270 !! or zero not to update the properties.
1271 type(MOM_control_struct), intent(inout), target :: CS !< control structure from initialize_MOM
1272 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time_local !< End time of a segment, as a time type
1273 type(wave_parameters_CS), &
1274 optional, pointer :: Waves !< Container for wave related parameters; the
1275 !! fields in Waves are intent in here.
1276
1277 ! local variables
1278 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G => NULL() ! pointer to a structure containing
1279 ! metrics and related information
1280 type(verticalGrid_type), pointer :: GV => NULL() ! Pointer to the vertical grid structure
1281 type(unit_scale_type), pointer :: US => NULL() ! Pointer to a structure containing
1282 ! various unit conversion factors
1283 type(MOM_diag_IDs), pointer :: IDs => NULL() ! A structure with the diagnostic IDs.
1284 real, dimension(:,:,:), pointer :: &
1285 u => NULL(), & ! u : zonal velocity component [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
1286 v => NULL(), & ! v : meridional velocity component [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
1287 h => NULL() ! h : layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1288
1289 type(time_type) :: Time_end_diag ! End time of a diagnostic segment, as a time type
1290 logical :: calc_dtbt ! Indicates whether the dynamically adjusted
1291 ! barotropic time step needs to be updated.
1292 logical :: showCallTree
1293
1294 integer :: i, j, k, is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, nz
1295 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
1296
1297720 G => CS%G ; GV => CS%GV ; US => CS%US ; IDs => CS%IDs
1298720 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke
1299720 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
1300720 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
1301720 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
1302720 u => CS%u ; v => CS%v ; h => CS%h
1303720 showCallTree = callTree_showQuery()
1304
1305720 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_dynamics)
1306
1307720 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_stoch)
1308720 if (CS%use_stochastic_EOS) call MOM_stoch_eos_run(G, u, v, dt, Time_local, CS%stoch_eos_CS)
1309720 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_stoch)
1310
1311720 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_varT)
1312720 if (CS%use_stochastic_EOS) then
13130 call MOM_calc_varT(G, GV, US, h, CS%tv, CS%stoch_eos_CS, dt)
13140 if (associated(CS%tv%varT)) call pass_var(CS%tv%varT, G%Domain, clock=id_clock_pass, halo=1)
1315 endif

13460 CS%tv%valid_SpV_halo = min(G%Domain%nihalo, G%Domain%njhalo)

1357 ! Whenever thickness changes let the diag manager know, target grids

13590 call diag_update_remap_grids(CS%diag)

13880 if (CS%dt_obc_seg_period == 0.0) CS%OBC%update_OBC_seg_data = .true.
13890 if (CS%dt_obc_seg_period > 0.0) then
13900 if (Time_local >= CS%dt_obc_seg_time) then
13910 CS%OBC%update_OBC_seg_data = .true.
13920 CS%dt_obc_seg_time = CS%dt_obc_seg_time + CS%dt_obc_seg_interval
1393 endif

1395 endif
1396 ! if (CS%debug_OBCs .and. associated(CS%OBC)) call chksum_OBC_segments(CS%OBC, G, GV, US, 3)

1398720 if (CS%do_dynamics .and. CS%split) then !--------------------------- start SPLIT
1399 ! This section uses a split time stepping scheme for the dynamic equations,
1400 ! basically the stacked shallow water equations with viscosity.

1402720 calc_dtbt = .false.

14060 calc_dtbt = .true.

14300 elseif (CS%do_dynamics) then ! ------------------------------------ not SPLIT
1431 ! This section uses an unsplit stepping scheme for the dynamic
1432 ! equations; basically the stacked shallow water equations with viscosity.
1433 ! Because the time step is limited by CFL restrictions on the external
1434 ! gravity waves, the unsplit is usually much less efficient that the split
1435 ! approaches. But because of its simplicity, the unsplit method is very
1436 ! useful for debugging purposes.
1437
14380 if (CS%use_RK2) then

14540 CS%stoch_CS, Waves=Waves)

1462720 if (CS%accumulate_resolved_flux) then

14640 do k=1,nz

1467 enddo ; enddo

14690 CS%vhtr_resolved(i,J,k) = CS%vhtr_resolved(i,J,k) + CS%vh(i,J,k)*dt
1470 enddo ; enddo
1471 enddo

1474720 if (CS%use_particles .and. CS%do_dynamics .and. (.not. CS%use_uh_particles)) then

1476 "Thickness_diffuse_first is true and use_uh_particles is false. "//&
14770 "This is usually a bad combination.")

14810 CS%tv, dt, CS%use_uh_particles)
14820 call particles_to_z_space(CS%particles, h)

1498 ! It is mostly for debugging, and perhaps doesn't need to hang
1499 ! around permanently.

1556720 if (CS%mixedlayer_restrat) then

15580 if (CS%debug) then
15590 call hchksum(h,"Pre-mixedlayer_restrat h", G%HI, haloshift=1, unscale=GV%H_to_MKS)
1560 call uvchksum("Pre-mixedlayer_restrat uhtr", &
15610 CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr, G%HI, haloshift=0, unscale=GV%H_to_MKS*US%L_to_m**2)
1562 endif

15670 CS%visc%sfc_buoy_flx, CS%VarMix, G, GV, US, CS%mixedlayer_restrat_CSp, CS%visc%Lam2)
1568 else
15690 call MOM_error(FATAL,'step_MOM_dynamics:CS%visc%Lam2 not associated')
1570 endif
1571 else
1572 call mixedlayer_restrat(h, CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr, CS%tv, forces, dt, CS%visc%MLD, CS%visc%h_ML, &
15730 CS%visc%sfc_buoy_flx, CS%VarMix, G, GV, US, CS%mixedlayer_restrat_CSp)
1574 endif
15750 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_ml_restrat)
15760 call pass_var(h, G%Domain, clock=id_clock_pass, halo=CS%dyn_h_stencil)
1577 !$omp target update to(h, CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr)
15780 if (CS%debug) then
15790 call hchksum(h,"Post-mixedlayer_restrat h", G%HI, haloshift=1, unscale=GV%H_to_MKS)
1580 call uvchksum("Post-mixedlayer_restrat [uv]htr", &
15810 CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr, G%HI, haloshift=0, unscale=GV%H_to_MKS*US%L_to_m**2)

1586 ! for vertical remapping may need to be regenerated.
1587720 call diag_update_remap_grids(CS%diag)

1589720 if (CS%useMEKE .and. CS%MEKE_in_dynamics) then

1591 !$omp target update from(CS%visc%bbl_thick_u, CS%visc%bbl_thick_v)
1592 !$omp target update from(CS%visc%kv_bbl_u, CS%visc%kv_bbl_v)
1593 call step_forward_MEKE(CS%MEKE, h, CS%VarMix%SN_u, CS%VarMix%SN_v, &
1594 CS%visc, dt, G, GV, US, CS%MEKE_CSp, CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr, &
15950 CS%u, CS%v, CS%tv, Time_local)
1596 !$omp target update to(u, v)
1597 endif
1598720 call disable_averaging(CS%diag)
1599
1600 ! Advance the dynamics time by dt.
1601720 CS%t_dyn_rel_adv = CS%t_dyn_rel_adv + dt
1602
1603720 if (CS%use_particles .and. CS%do_dynamics .and. CS%use_uh_particles .and. &
1604 CS%uh_particles_bug) then
1605 !$omp target update to(h, CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr)
1606 ! Run particles using thickness-weighted velocity
1607 call particles_run(CS%particles, Time_local, CS%uhtr, CS%vhtr, CS%h, &
16080 CS%tv, CS%t_dyn_rel_adv, CS%use_uh_particles)
1609 endif
1610
1611720 CS%n_dyn_steps_in_adv = CS%n_dyn_steps_in_adv + 1
1612720 if (CS%alternate_first_direction) then
16130 call set_first_direction(G, MODULO(G%first_direction+1,2))
16140 CS%first_dir_restart = real(G%first_direction)

1618720 CS%t_dyn_rel_thermo = CS%t_dyn_rel_thermo + dt
1619720 if (abs(CS%t_dyn_rel_thermo) < 1e-6*dt) CS%t_dyn_rel_thermo = 0.0
1620720 CS%t_dyn_rel_diag = CS%t_dyn_rel_diag + dt

1623
1624 ! Diagnostic finalization
1625
1626720 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_other)
1627720 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_diagnostics)

1629720 call enable_averages(dt, Time_local, CS%diag)

1631 ! These diagnostics are available after every time dynamics step.

16340 call post_data(IDs%id_u, u, CS%diag)
1635 endif
1636720 if (IDs%id_v > 0) then
1637 !$omp target update from(v)
16380 call post_data(IDs%id_v, v, CS%diag)
1639 endif
1640720 if (IDs%id_h > 0) then

16420 call post_data(IDs%id_h, h, CS%diag)
1643 endif

1646
1647720 call disable_averaging(CS%diag)

2004!> ALE_regridding_and_remapping does regridding (the generation of a new grid) and remapping
2005!! (from the old grid to the new grid). This is done after the themrodynamic step.
20060subroutine ALE_regridding_and_remapping(CS, G, GV, US, u, v, h, tv, dtdia, Time_end_thermo)

2232!> step_offline is the main driver for running tracers offline in MOM6. This has been primarily
2233!! developed with ALE configurations in mind. Some work has been done in isopycnal configuration, but
2234!! the work is very preliminary. Some more detail about this capability along with some of the subroutines
2235!! called here can be found in tracers/MOM_offline_control.F90
22360subroutine step_offline(forces, fluxes, sfc_state, Time_start, time_interval, CS)
2237 type(mech_forcing), intent(in) :: forces !< A structure with the driving mechanical forces
2238 type(forcing), intent(inout) :: fluxes !< pointers to forcing fields
2239 type(surface), intent(inout) :: sfc_state !< surface ocean state
2240 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time_start !< starting time of a segment, as a time type
2241 real, intent(in) :: time_interval !< time interval [T ~> s]
2242 type(MOM_control_struct), intent(inout) :: CS !< control structure from initialize_MOM
2243
2244 ! Local pointers
2245 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G => NULL() ! Pointer to a structure containing
2246 ! metrics and related information
2247 type(verticalGrid_type), pointer :: GV => NULL() ! Pointer to structure containing information
2248 ! about the vertical grid
2249 type(unit_scale_type), pointer :: US => NULL() ! Pointer to a structure containing
2250 ! various unit conversion factors
2251
2252 logical :: first_iter !< True if this is the first time step_offline has been called in a given interval
2253 logical :: last_iter !< True if this is the last time step_tracer is to be called in an offline interval
2254 logical :: do_vertical !< If enough time has elapsed, do the diabatic tracer sources/sinks
2255 logical :: adv_converged !< True if all the horizontal fluxes have been used
2256
22570 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: h_new ! Layer thicknesses after regridding [H ~> m or kg m-2]
22580 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: dzRegrid ! The change in grid interface positions due to regridding,
2259 ! in the same units as thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2260 real :: dt_offline ! The offline timestep for advection [T ~> s]
2261 real :: dt_offline_vertical ! The offline timestep for vertical fluxes and remapping [T ~> s]
2262 logical :: skip_diffusion
2263
2264 type(time_type), pointer :: accumulated_time => NULL()
2265 type(time_type), pointer :: vertical_time => NULL()
2266 integer :: dynamics_stencil ! The computational stencil for the calculations
2267 ! in the dynamic core.
2268 integer :: i, j, k, is, ie, js, je, isd, ied, jsd, jed, nz
2269
2270 ! 3D pointers
2271 real, dimension(:,:,:), pointer :: &
2272 uhtr => NULL(), & ! Accumulated zonal thickness fluxes to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
2273 vhtr => NULL(), & ! Accumulated meridional thickness fluxes to advect tracers [H L2 ~> m3 or kg]
2274 eatr => NULL(), & ! Layer entrainment rates across the interface above [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2275 ebtr => NULL(), & ! Layer entrainment rates across the interface below [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2276 h_end => NULL() ! Layer thicknesses at the end of a step [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2277
2278 type(time_type) :: Time_end ! End time of a segment, as a time type
2279
2280 ! Grid-related pointer assignments
22810 G => CS%G ; GV => CS%GV ; US => CS%US
2282
22830 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke
22840 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
2285
22860 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_offline_tracer)
2287 call extract_offline_main(CS%offline_CSp, uhtr, vhtr, eatr, ebtr, h_end, accumulated_time, &
22880 vertical_time, dt_offline, dt_offline_vertical, skip_diffusion)
22890 Time_end = increment_date(Time_start, seconds=floor(US%T_to_s*time_interval+0.001))
2290
22910 call enable_averages(time_interval, Time_end, CS%diag)
2292
2293 ! Check to see if this is the first iteration of the offline interval
22940 first_iter = (accumulated_time == real_to_time(0.0))
2295
2296 ! Check to see if vertical tracer functions should be done
22970 do_vertical = (first_iter .or. (accumulated_time >= vertical_time))
22980 if (do_vertical) vertical_time = accumulated_time + real_to_time(dt_offline_vertical, unscale=US%T_to_s)
2299
2300 ! Increment the amount of time elapsed since last read and check if it's time to roll around
23010 accumulated_time = accumulated_time + real_to_time(time_interval, unscale=US%T_to_s)
2302
23030 last_iter = (accumulated_time >= real_to_time(dt_offline, unscale=US%T_to_s))
2304
23050 if (CS%use_ALE_algorithm) then
2306 ! If this is the first iteration in the offline timestep, then we need to read in fields and
2307 ! perform the main advection.
23080 if (first_iter) then
23090 call MOM_mesg("Reading in new offline fields")
2310 ! Read in new transport and other fields
2311 ! call update_transport_from_files(G, GV, CS%offline_CSp, h_end, eatr, ebtr, uhtr, vhtr, &
2312 ! CS%tv%T, CS%tv%S, fluxes, CS%use_ALE_algorithm)
2313 ! call update_transport_from_arrays(CS%offline_CSp)
23140 call update_offline_fields(CS%offline_CSp, G, GV, US, CS%h, fluxes, CS%use_ALE_algorithm)
2315
2316 ! Apply any fluxes into the ocean
23170 call offline_fw_fluxes_into_ocean(G, GV, CS%offline_CSp, fluxes, CS%h)
2318
23190 if (.not.CS%diabatic_first) then
2320 call offline_advection_ale(fluxes, Time_start, time_interval, G, GV, US, CS%offline_CSp, &
23210 id_clock_ALE, CS%h, uhtr, vhtr, converged=adv_converged)
2322
2323 ! Redistribute any remaining transport
23240 call offline_redistribute_residual(CS%offline_CSp, G, GV, US, CS%h, uhtr, vhtr, adv_converged)
2325
2326 ! Perform offline diffusion if requested
23270 if (.not. skip_diffusion) then
23280 if (CS%VarMix%use_variable_mixing) then
23290 call pass_var(CS%h, G%Domain)
23300 call calc_resoln_function(CS%h, CS%tv, G, GV, US, CS%VarMix, CS%MEKE, CS%OBC, dt_offline)
23310 call calc_depth_function(G, CS%VarMix)
23320 call calc_slope_functions(CS%h, CS%tv, dt_offline, G, GV, US, CS%VarMix, OBC=CS%OBC)
2333 endif
2334 call tracer_hordiff(CS%h, dt_offline, CS%MEKE, CS%VarMix, CS%visc, G, GV, US, &
23350 CS%tracer_diff_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, CS%tv)
2336 endif
2337 endif
2338 endif
2339 ! The functions related to column physics of tracers is performed separately in ALE mode
23400 if (do_vertical) then
2341 call offline_diabatic_ale(fluxes, Time_start, Time_end, G, GV, US, CS%offline_CSp, &
23420 CS%h, CS%tv, eatr, ebtr)
2343 endif
2344
2345 ! Last thing that needs to be done is the final ALE remapping
23460 if (last_iter) then
23470 if (CS%diabatic_first) then
2348 call offline_advection_ale(fluxes, Time_start, time_interval, G, GV, US, CS%offline_CSp, &
23490 id_clock_ALE, CS%h, uhtr, vhtr, converged=adv_converged)
2350
2351 ! Redistribute any remaining transport and perform the remaining advection
23520 call offline_redistribute_residual(CS%offline_CSp, G, GV, US, CS%h, uhtr, vhtr, adv_converged)
2353 ! Perform offline diffusion if requested
23540 if (.not. skip_diffusion) then
23550 if (CS%VarMix%use_variable_mixing) then
23560 call pass_var(CS%h, G%Domain)
23570 call calc_resoln_function(CS%h, CS%tv, G, GV, US, CS%VarMix, CS%MEKE, CS%OBC, dt_offline)
23580 call calc_depth_function(G, CS%VarMix)
23590 call calc_slope_functions(CS%h, CS%tv, dt_offline, G, GV, US, CS%VarMix, OBC=CS%OBC)
2360 endif
2361 call tracer_hordiff(CS%h, dt_offline, CS%MEKE, CS%VarMix, CS%visc, G, GV, US, &
23620 CS%tracer_diff_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, CS%tv)
2363 endif
2364 endif
2365
23660 call MOM_mesg("Last iteration of offline interval")
2367
2368 ! Apply freshwater fluxes out of the ocean
23690 call offline_fw_fluxes_out_ocean(G, GV, CS%offline_CSp, fluxes, CS%h)
2370 ! These diagnostic can be used to identify which grid points did not converge within
2371 ! the specified number of advection sub iterations
23720 call post_offline_convergence_diags(G, GV, CS%offline_CSp, CS%h, h_end, uhtr, vhtr)
2373
2374 ! Call ALE one last time to make sure that tracers are remapped onto the layer thicknesses
2375 ! stored from the forward run
23760 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_ALE)
2377
2378 ! Do any necessary adjustments ot the state prior to remapping.
23790 call pre_ALE_adjustments(G, GV, US, h_end, CS%tv, CS%tracer_Reg, CS%ALE_CSp)
2380
23810 allocate(h_new(isd:ied, jsd:jed, nz), source=0.0)
23820 allocate(dzRegrid(isd:ied, jsd:jed, nz+1), source=0.0)
2383
2384 ! Generate the new grid based on the tracer grid at the end of the interval.
23850 call ALE_regrid(G, GV, US, h_end, h_new, dzRegrid, CS%tv, CS%ALE_CSp)
2386
2387 ! Remap the tracers from the previous tracer grid onto the new grid. The thicknesses that
2388 ! are used are intended to ensure that in the case where transports don't quite conserve,
2389 ! the offline layer thicknesses do not drift too far away from the online model.
23900 call ALE_remap_tracers(CS%ALE_CSp, G, GV, CS%h, h_new, CS%tracer_Reg, debug=CS%debug)
23910 if (allocated(CS%tv%SpV_avg)) CS%tv%valid_SpV_halo = -1 ! Record that SpV_avg is no longer valid.
2392
2393 ! Update the tracer grid.
23940 do k=1,nz ; do j=js-1,je+1 ; do i=is-1,ie+1
23950 CS%h(i,j,k) = h_new(i,j,k)
2396 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
2397
23980 deallocate(h_new, dzRegrid)
2399
24000 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_ALE)
24010 call pass_var(CS%h, G%Domain)
2402 endif
2403 else ! NON-ALE MODE...NOT WELL TESTED
2404 call MOM_error(WARNING, &
24050 "Offline tracer mode in non-ALE configuration has not been thoroughly tested")
2406 ! Note that for the layer mode case, the calls to tracer sources and sinks is embedded in
2407 ! main_offline_advection_layer. Warning: this may not be appropriate for tracers that
2408 ! exchange with the atmosphere
24090 if (abs(time_interval - dt_offline) > 1.0e-6*US%s_to_T) then
2410 call MOM_error(FATAL, &
24110 "For offline tracer mode in a non-ALE configuration, dt_offline must equal time_interval")
2412 endif
24130 call update_offline_fields(CS%offline_CSp, G, GV, US, CS%h, fluxes, CS%use_ALE_algorithm)
2414 call offline_advection_layer(fluxes, Time_start, time_interval, G, GV, US, CS%offline_CSp, &
24150 CS%h, eatr, ebtr, uhtr, vhtr)
2416 ! Perform offline diffusion if requested
24170 if (.not. skip_diffusion) then
2418 call tracer_hordiff(h_end, dt_offline, CS%MEKE, CS%VarMix, CS%visc, G, GV, US, &
24190 CS%tracer_diff_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, CS%tv)
2420 endif
2421
24220 CS%h = h_end
2423
24240 call pass_var(CS%tv%T, G%Domain)
24250 call pass_var(CS%tv%S, G%Domain)
24260 call pass_var(CS%h, G%Domain)
2427
2428 endif
2429
2430 call adjust_ssh_for_p_atm(CS%tv, G, GV, US, CS%ave_ssh_ibc, forces%p_surf_SSH, &
24310 CS%calc_rho_for_sea_lev)
24320 call extract_surface_state(CS, sfc_state)
2433
24340 call disable_averaging(CS%diag)
24350 call pass_var(CS%tv%T, G%Domain)
24360 call pass_var(CS%tv%S, G%Domain)
24370 call pass_var(CS%h, G%Domain)
2438
24390 fluxes%fluxes_used = .true.
2440
2441 ! Update derived thermodynamic quantities.
24420 if (allocated(CS%tv%SpV_avg)) then
24430 dynamics_stencil = min(3, G%Domain%nihalo, G%Domain%njhalo)
24440 call calc_derived_thermo(CS%tv, CS%h, G, GV, US, halo=dynamics_stencil)
2445 endif
2446
24470 if (last_iter) then
24480 accumulated_time = real_to_time(0.0)
2449 endif
2450
24510 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_offline_tracer)
2452
24530end subroutine step_offline
2454

27291 do_not_log=.true., default=.true.)
27301 if (present(offline_tracer_mode)) then ! Only read this parameter in enabled modes
2731 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "OFFLINE_TRACER_MODE", CS%offline_tracer_mode, &

2738 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "ADVECT_TS", advect_TS, &
2739 "If True, advect temperature and salinity horizontally "//&
2740 "If False, T/S are registered for advection. "//&
2741 "This is intended only to be used in offline tracer mode, "//&
2742 "and is by default false in that case", &
27430 default=.false. )
2744 endif
2745 endif
2746 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "USE_REGRIDDING", CS%use_ALE_algorithm, &
2747 "If True, use the ALE algorithm (regridding/remapping). "//&
27481 "If False, use the layered isopycnal algorithm.", default=.false. )
2749 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "REMAP_UV_USING_OLD_ALG", CS%remap_uv_using_old_alg, &
2750 "If true, uses the old remapping-via-a-delta-z method for "//&
2751 "remapping u and v. If false, uses the new method that remaps "//&
2752 "between grids described by an old and new thickness.", &
27531 default=.false., do_not_log=.not.CS%use_ALE_algorithm)
2754 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "REMAP_AUXILIARY_VARS", CS%remap_aux_vars, &
2755 "If true, apply ALE remapping to all of the auxiliary 3-dimensional "//&

2758 "The default should be changed to true.", default=.false., &
27591 do_not_log=.not.CS%use_ALE_algorithm)
2760 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "BULKMIXEDLAYER", bulkmixedlayer, &

2765 "BULKMIXEDLAYER can not be used with USE_REGRIDDING. "//&
2766 "The default is influenced by ENABLE_THERMODYNAMICS.", &

35390 allocate(CS%mass_shelf(isd:ied, jsd:jed), source=0.0)
35400 call ice_shelf_query(ice_shelf_CSp, G, CS%frac_shelf_h, CS%mass_shelf)
3541 ! MOM_initialize_state is using the unrotated metric
35420 call rotate_array(CS%frac_shelf_h, -turns, frac_shelf_in)
35430 call rotate_array(CS%mass_shelf, -turns, mass_shelf_in)
3544 call MOM_initialize_state(u_in, v_in, h_in, CS%tv, Time, G_in, GV, US, &
3545 param_file, dirs, restart_CSp, CS%ALE_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, &
3546 sponge_in_CSp, ALE_sponge_in_CSp, oda_incupd_in_CSp, OBC_in, Time_in, &
35470 frac_shelf_h=frac_shelf_in, mass_shelf=mass_shelf_in)
3548 else

3550 param_file, dirs, restart_CSp, CS%ALE_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, &
35510 sponge_in_CSp, ALE_sponge_in_CSp, oda_incupd_in_CSp, OBC_in, Time_in)
3552 endif

35540 if (use_temperature) then
35550 CS%tv%T => CS%T
35560 CS%tv%S => CS%S

35600 if (CS%first_dir_restart > -1.0) then
35610 call set_first_direction(G, modulo(NINT(CS%first_dir_restart) + turns, 2))
3562 else
35630 CS%first_dir_restart = real(modulo(first_direction, 2))
3564 endif

35670 turns, CS%u, CS%v, CS%h, CS%T, CS%S)

35690 if (associated(sponge_in_CSp)) then

35860 deallocate(S_in)
3587 endif

3590
3591 else ! The model is being run without grid rotation. This is true of all production runs.
35921 if (use_ice_shelf) then

35940 dirs%output_directory, calve_ice_shelf_bergs=point_calving)

3598 call MOM_initialize_state(CS%u, CS%v, CS%h, CS%tv, Time, G, GV, US, &
3599 param_file, dirs, restart_CSp, CS%ALE_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, &
3600 CS%sponge_CSp, CS%ALE_sponge_CSp, CS%oda_incupd_CSp, CS%OBC, Time_in, &
36010 frac_shelf_h=CS%frac_shelf_h, mass_shelf=CS%mass_shelf, OBC_for_bug=CS%OBC)
3602 else

3604 param_file, dirs, restart_CSp, CS%ALE_CSp, CS%tracer_Reg, &
36051 CS%sponge_CSp, CS%ALE_sponge_CSp, CS%oda_incupd_CSp, CS%OBC, Time_in, OBC_for_bug=CS%OBC)
3606 endif

3608 ! Reset the first direction if it was found in a restart file.
36091 if (CS%first_dir_restart > -1.0) then
36100 call set_first_direction(G, NINT(CS%first_dir_restart))
3611 else
36121 CS%first_dir_restart = real(modulo(first_direction, 2))
3613 endif
3614 endif

3616 ! Allocate any derived densities or other equation of state derived fields.
36171 if (.not.(GV%Boussinesq .or. GV%semi_Boussinesq)) then

37400 if (use_temperature) then
37410 call hchksum(CS%tv%T, "Post ALE adjust init cond T", G%HI, haloshift=2, unscale=US%C_to_degC)
37420 call hchksum(CS%tv%S, "Post ALE adjust init cond S", G%HI, haloshift=2, unscale=US%S_to_ppt)
3743 endif
3744 endif
3745 endif
37461 if ( CS%use_ALE_algorithm ) then
37470 call ALE_set_extrap_boundaries (param_file, CS%ALE_CSp)

37490 call ALE_updateVerticalGridType( CS%ALE_CSp, GV )

37600 call create_group_pass(pass_uv_T_S_h, CS%tv%S, G%Domain, halo=dynamics_stencil)
3761 endif

3783 endif
37841 if (present(diag_ptr)) diag_ptr => CS%diag

3829
38301 if (CS%use_dbclient) call database_comms_init(param_file, CS%dbcomms_CS)

38321 restart_CSp, CS%MEKE_in_dynamics)

38721 cont_stencil=CS%cont_stencil, dyn_h_stencil=CS%dyn_h_stencil)

3874 ! A reset period no longer than dt is equivalent to recalculating every step.
38751 if (CS%dtbt_reset_period > 0.0 .and. CS%dtbt_reset_period <= CS%dt) &
38760 CS%dtbt_reset_period = 0.0
38771 if (CS%dtbt_reset_period > 0.0) then

38790 if (calc_dtbt) then
3880 ! No restart DTBT (not found or new run) or DTBT_RESTART_BUG=True: set to the most recent
3881 ! multiple of the interval before or equal to current time, so the set_dtbt is called on
3882 ! the first step.

38840 ((Time - Time_init) / CS%dtbt_reset_interval)
3885 else
3886 ! Restart DTBT available: defer to the next multiple after current time so the first step
3887 ! uses the restart value unless a reset is naturally due.
3888 CS%dtbt_reset_time = Time_init + CS%dtbt_reset_interval * &
38890 ((Time - Time_init) / CS%dtbt_reset_interval + 1)
3890 endif
3891 endif
38920 elseif (CS%use_RK2) then

38970 CS%ntrunc, cont_stencil=CS%cont_stencil, dyn_h_stencil=CS%dyn_h_stencil)
3898 else

3900 US, param_file, diag, CS%dyn_unsplit_CSp, &

39030 CS%ntrunc, cont_stencil=CS%cont_stencil, dyn_h_stencil=CS%dyn_h_stencil)
3904 endif
39051 CS%dyn_h_stencil = max(2, CS%dyn_h_stencil)

3907 ! When IGNORE_DT_OBC_SEG_UPDATE_OBGC is true, BGC OBC updates happen every tracer advection step.
39081 if (associated(CS%OBC)) then
39090 if (CS%OBC%ignore_dt_obc_bgc) CS%dt_obc_seg_period = 0.0
3910 endif
3911
3912 ! Set the next time to update OBC segment BGC tracer data
39131 if (associated(CS%OBC) .and. (CS%dt_obc_seg_period > 0.0)) then
39140 if (CS%dt_obc_seg_period > CS%dt_tr_adv) then
39150 if (new_sim) then
3916 call MOM_error(WARNING, "DT_OBC_SEG_UPDATE_OBGC > DT_TRACER_ADVECT: this run will "//&
3917 "proceed normally, but any restart from it will fail with a FATAL error because "//&
3918 "BGC OBC external data is not saved in restart files. "//&
39190 "Set DT_OBC_SEG_UPDATE_OBGC = DT_TRACER_ADVECT or 0 until this is fixed.")
3920 else
3921 call MOM_error(FATAL, "DT_OBC_SEG_UPDATE_OBGC > DT_TRACER_ADVECT is not supported "//&
3922 "for restart runs: BGC OBC external data is not saved in restart files, so the "//&
3923 "first tracer advection step after restart may use incorrect boundary data. "//&
39240 "Set DT_OBC_SEG_UPDATE_OBGC = DT_TRACER_ADVECT or 0 until this is fixed.")
3925 endif
3926 endif
39270 CS%dt_obc_seg_interval = real_to_time(CS%dt_obc_seg_period, unscale=US%T_to_s)
39280 if (OBC_bgc_time_ref_bug) then
39290 CS%dt_obc_seg_time = Time + CS%dt_obc_seg_interval
3930 else
3931 ! Set to the next update point after current time, so that %t read from initialization is
3932 ! not overwritten. Note that even though this line by itself is correct, there are still
3933 ! issue with restart runs, as external data %t is not saved in restart files.
3934 CS%dt_obc_seg_time = Time_init + CS%dt_obc_seg_interval * &
39350 ((Time - Time_init) / CS%dt_obc_seg_interval + 1)
3936 endif
3937 endif
3938
39391 call callTree_waypoint("dynamics initialized (initialize_MOM)")
3940
3941 CS%mixedlayer_restrat = mixedlayer_restrat_init(Time, G, GV, US, param_file, diag, &
39421 CS%mixedlayer_restrat_CSp, restart_CSp)
3943
39441 if (GV%Boussinesq .and. associated(CS%visc%h_ML)) then
3945 ! This is here to allow for a transition of restart files between model versions.
3946 call get_param(param_file, "MOM", "MLE_USE_PBL_MLD", MLE_use_PBL_MLD, &
39470 default=.false., do_not_log=.true.)
39480 if (MLE_use_PBL_MLD .and. .not.query_initialized(CS%visc%h_ML, "h_ML", restart_CSp) .and. &
3949 associated(CS%visc%MLD)) then
39500 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie ; CS%visc%h_ML(i,j) = GV%Z_to_H * CS%visc%MLD(i,j) ; enddo ; enddo
3951 endif
3952 endif
3953
39541 if (CS%mixedlayer_restrat) then
39550 if (.not.(bulkmixedlayer .or. CS%use_ALE_algorithm)) &
39560 call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM: MIXEDLAYER_RESTRAT true requires a boundary layer scheme.")
3957 ! When DIABATIC_FIRST=False and using CS%visc%ML in mixedlayer_restrat we need to update after a restart
39580 if (.not. CS%diabatic_first .and. associated(CS%visc%MLD)) &
39590 call pass_var(CS%visc%MLD, G%domain, halo=1)
39600 if (.not. CS%diabatic_first .and. associated(CS%visc%h_ML)) &
39610 call pass_var(CS%visc%h_ML, G%domain, halo=1)
3962 endif
3963
3964 call MOM_diagnostics_init(MOM_internal_state, CS%ADp, CS%CDp, Time, G, GV, US, &
39651 param_file, diag, CS%diagnostics_CSp, CS%tv)
39661 call diag_copy_diag_to_storage(CS%diag_pre_sync, CS%h, CS%diag)
3967
3968
39691 if (CS%adiabatic) then
3970 call adiabatic_driver_init(Time, G, param_file, diag, CS%diabatic_CSp, &
39711 CS%tracer_flow_CSp)
3972 else
3973 call diabatic_driver_init(Time, G, GV, US, param_file, CS%use_ALE_algorithm, diag, &
3974 CS%ADp, CS%CDp, CS%diabatic_CSp, CS%tracer_flow_CSp, &
39750 CS%sponge_CSp, CS%ALE_sponge_CSp, CS%oda_incupd_CSp, CS%int_tide_CSp)

3979

39811 if (CS%split .and. fpmix) then

3983 endif

39851 if (associated(CS%sponge_CSp)) &
39860 call init_sponge_diags(Time, G, GV, US, diag, CS%sponge_CSp)
3987
39881 if (associated(CS%oda_incupd_CSp)) &

3990
39911 call tracer_advect_init(Time, G, US, param_file, diag, CS%tracer_adv_CSp)
3992 call tracer_hor_diff_init(Time, G, GV, US, param_file, diag, CS%tv%eqn_of_state, CS%diabatic_CSp, &
39931 CS%tracer_diff_CSp)
3994
39951 call lock_tracer_registry(CS%tracer_Reg)
39961 call callTree_waypoint("tracer registry now locked (initialize_MOM)")
3997

39991 call register_surface_diags(Time, G, US, CS%sfc_IDs, CS%diag, CS%tv)
40001 call register_diags(Time, G, GV, US, CS%IDs, CS%diag)
40011 call register_transport_diags(Time, G, GV, US, CS%transport_IDs, CS%diag)
40021 call extract_diabatic_member(CS%diabatic_CSp, use_KPP=use_KPP)
4003 call register_tracer_diagnostics(CS%tracer_Reg, CS%h, Time, diag, G, GV, US, &
40041 CS%use_ALE_algorithm, use_KPP)
40051 if (CS%use_ALE_algorithm) then
40060 call ALE_register_diags(Time, G, GV, US, diag, CS%ALE_CSp)
4007 endif
4008
4009 ! determine if we need to accumulate resolved transports for tracer advection diagnostics
40101 do m = 1,CS%tracer_Reg%ntr
40110 if (CS%tracer_Reg%Tr(m)%id_adx_resolved > 0 .or. &
4012 CS%tracer_Reg%Tr(m)%id_ady_resolved > 0) then
40130 CS%accumulate_resolved_flux = .true.
40140 CS%do_resolved_advection = .true.
4015 endif
40160 if (CS%tracer_Reg%Tr(m)%id_adx_param > 0 .or. &
40171 CS%tracer_Reg%Tr(m)%id_ady_param > 0) then
40180 CS%accumulate_resolved_flux = .true.
40190 CS%do_param_advection = .true.
4020 endif
4021 enddo
40221 if (CS%accumulate_resolved_flux) then
40230 allocate(CS%uhtr_resolved(IsdB:IedB,jsd:jed,nz), source=0.0)
40240 allocate(CS%vhtr_resolved(isd:ied,JsdB:JedB,nz), source=0.0)
4025 endif

4028 ! Do any necessary halo updates on any auxiliary variables that have been initialized.
40291 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_pass_init)

4032

40401 CS%ALE_sponge_CSp, CS%tv)
40411 if (present(tracer_flow_CSp)) tracer_flow_CSp => CS%tracer_flow_CSp
4042

40601 if (associated(CS%OBC)) then

41061 CS%nstep_tot = 0

41091 CS%ntrunc, Time_init, CS%sum_output_CSp)

4112 CS%write_IC = save_IC .and. &
4113 .not.((dirs%input_filename(1:1) == 'r') .and. &
41141 (LEN_TRIM(dirs%input_filename) == 1))

4118 endif
4119

41211 call stochastics_init(CS%dt_therm, CS%G, CS%GV, US, CS%stoch_CS, param_file, diag, Time)
4122
41231 call callTree_leave("initialize_MOM()")
41241 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_init) ; call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_ocean)

412613end subroutine initialize_MOM

4128!> Finishes initializing MOM and writes out the initial conditions.
41291subroutine finish_MOM_initialization(Time, dirs, CS)
4130 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time !< model time, used in this routine
4131 type(directories), intent(in) :: dirs !< structure with directory paths
4132 type(MOM_control_struct), intent(inout) :: CS !< MOM control structure
4133
4134 type(ocean_grid_type), pointer :: G => NULL() ! pointer to a structure containing
4135 ! metrics and related information
4136 type(verticalGrid_type), pointer :: GV => NULL() ! Pointer to the vertical grid structure
4137 type(unit_scale_type), pointer :: US => NULL() ! Pointer to a structure containing
4138 ! various unit conversion factors
4139 type(MOM_restart_CS), pointer :: restart_CSp_tmp => NULL()
41401 real, allocatable :: z_interface(:,:,:) ! Interface heights [Z ~> m]
4141
41421 call cpu_clock_begin(id_clock_init)
41431 call callTree_enter("finish_MOM_initialization()")
4144
4145 ! Pointers for convenience
41461 G => CS%G ; GV => CS%GV ; US => CS%US
4147
41481 if (CS%use_particles) then
41490 call particles_init(CS%particles, G, CS%Time, CS%dt_therm, CS%u, CS%v, CS%h)
4150 endif
4151
4152 ! Write initial conditions
41531 if (CS%write_IC) then
41540 allocate(restart_CSp_tmp)
41550 restart_CSp_tmp = CS%restart_CS
41560 call restart_registry_lock(restart_CSp_tmp, unlocked=.true.)
41570 allocate(z_interface(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1))
4158 !$omp target update to(CS%h)
4159 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: z_interface)
41600 call find_eta(CS%h, CS%tv, G, GV, US, z_interface, dZref=G%Z_ref)
4161 !$omp target exit data map(from: z_interface)
4162 call register_restart_field(z_interface, "eta", .true., restart_CSp_tmp, &
41630 "Interface heights", "meter", z_grid='i', conversion=US%Z_to_m)
4164 ! NOTE: write_ic=.true. routes routine to fms2 IO write_initial_conditions interface
4165 call save_restart(dirs%output_directory, Time, CS%G_in, &
41660 restart_CSp_tmp, filename=CS%IC_file, GV=GV, write_ic=.true.)
41670 deallocate(z_interface)
41680 deallocate(restart_CSp_tmp)
4169 endif
4170
4171 !$omp target update to(CS%u, CS%v, CS%h)
4172 call write_energy(CS%u, CS%v, CS%h, CS%tv, Time, 0, G, GV, US, &
41731 CS%sum_output_CSp, CS%tracer_flow_CSp)
4174
41751 call callTree_leave("finish_MOM_initialization()")
41761 call cpu_clock_end(id_clock_init)
4177
41781end subroutine finish_MOM_initialization

4246!> Set the fields that are needed for bitwise identical restarting
4247!! the time stepping scheme. In addition to those specified here
4248!! directly, there may be fields related to the forcing or to the
4249!! barotropic solver that are needed; these are specified in sub-
4250!! routines that are called from this one.
4251!!
4252!! This routine should be altered if there are any changes to the
4253!! time stepping scheme. The CHECK_RESTART facility may be used to
4254!! confirm that all needed restart fields have been included.
42551subroutine set_restart_fields(GV, US, param_file, CS, restart_CSp)
4256 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(inout) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
4257 type(unit_scale_type), intent(inout) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
4258 type(param_file_type), intent(in) :: param_file !< opened file for parsing to get parameters
4259 type(MOM_control_struct), intent(in) :: CS !< control structure set up by initialize_MOM
4260 type(MOM_restart_CS), pointer :: restart_CSp !< pointer to the restart control
4261 !! structure that will be used for MOM.
4262 ! Local variables
4263 logical :: use_ice_shelf ! Needed to determine whether to add CS%Hml to restarts
4264 character(len=48) :: thickness_units, flux_units
426511 type(vardesc) :: u_desc, v_desc
4266
42671 thickness_units = get_thickness_units(GV)
42681 flux_units = get_flux_units(GV)
4269
42701 if (associated(CS%tv%T)) &
4271 call register_restart_field(CS%tv%T, "Temp", .true., restart_CSp, &
42720 "Potential Temperature", "degC", conversion=US%C_to_degC)
42731 if (associated(CS%tv%S)) &
4274 call register_restart_field(CS%tv%S, "Salt", .true., restart_CSp, &
42750 "Salinity", "PPT", conversion=US%S_to_ppt)
4276
4277 call register_restart_field(CS%h, "h", .true., restart_CSp, &
42781 "Layer Thickness", thickness_units, conversion=GV%H_to_MKS)
4279
42806 u_desc = var_desc("u", "m s-1", "Zonal velocity", hor_grid='Cu')
42816 v_desc = var_desc("v", "m s-1", "Meridional velocity", hor_grid='Cv')
42821 call register_restart_pair(CS%u, CS%v, u_desc, v_desc, .true., restart_CSp, conversion=US%L_T_to_m_s)
4283
42841 if (associated(CS%tv%frazil)) &
4285 call register_restart_field(CS%tv%frazil, "frazil", .false., restart_CSp, &
4286 "Frazil heat flux into ocean", &
42870 "J m-2", conversion=US%Q_to_J_kg*US%RZ_to_kg_m2)
4288
42891 if (CS%interp_p_surf) then
4290 call register_restart_field(CS%p_surf_prev, "p_surf_prev", .false., restart_CSp, &
42910 "Previous ocean surface pressure", "Pa", conversion=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
4292 endif
4293
42941 if (associated(CS%tv%p_surf)) &
4295 call register_restart_field(CS%tv%p_surf, "p_surf_EOS", .false., restart_CSp, &
42960 "Ocean surface pressure used in EoS", "Pa", conversion=US%RL2_T2_to_Pa)
4297
4298 call register_restart_field(CS%ave_ssh_ibc, "ave_ssh", .false., restart_CSp, &
42991 "Time average sea surface height", "meter", conversion=US%Z_to_m)
4300
4301 ! hML is needed when using the ice shelf module
4302 call get_param(param_file, '', "ICE_SHELF", use_ice_shelf, default=.false., &
43031 do_not_log=.true.)
43041 if (use_ice_shelf .and. associated(CS%Hml)) then
4305 call register_restart_field(CS%Hml, "hML", .false., restart_CSp, &
43060 "Mixed layer thickness", "m", conversion=US%Z_to_m)
4307 endif
4308
4309 ! Register scalar unit conversion factors.
4310 call register_restart_field(CS%first_dir_restart, "First_direction", .false., restart_CSp, &
43111 "Indicator of the first direction in split calculations.", "nondim")
4312
431311end subroutine set_restart_fields
4314
4315!> Apply a correction to the sea surface height to compensate
4316!! for the atmospheric pressure (the inverse barometer).
4317360subroutine adjust_ssh_for_p_atm(tv, G, GV, US, ssh, p_atm, use_EOS)
4318 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(in) :: tv !< A structure pointing to various thermodynamic variables
4319 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< ocean grid structure
4320 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
4321 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
4322 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: ssh !< time mean surface height [Z ~> m]
4323 real, dimension(:,:), pointer :: p_atm !< Ocean surface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4324 logical, intent(in) :: use_EOS !< If true, calculate the density for
4325 !! the SSH correction using the equation of state.
4326
4327720 real :: Rho_conv(SZI_(G)) ! The density used to convert surface pressure to
4328 ! a corrected effective SSH [R ~> kg m-3].
4329 real :: IgR0 ! The SSH conversion factor from R L2 T-2 to Z [Z T2 R-1 L-2 ~> m Pa-1].
4330 logical :: calc_rho
4331 integer, dimension(2) :: EOSdom ! The i-computational domain for the equation of state
4332 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
4333
4334360 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
4335360 EOSdom(:) = EOS_domain(G%HI)
4336360 if (associated(p_atm)) then
4337 !$omp target update from(ssh)
43380 calc_rho = use_EOS .and. associated(tv%eqn_of_state)
4339 ! Correct the output sea surface height for the contribution from the ice pressure.
43400 do j=js,je
43410 if (calc_rho) then
4342 call calculate_density(tv%T(:,j,1), tv%S(:,j,1), 0.5*p_atm(:,j), Rho_conv, &
43430 tv%eqn_of_state, EOSdom)
43440 do i=is,ie
43450 IgR0 = 1.0 / (Rho_conv(i) * GV%g_Earth)
43460 ssh(i,j) = ssh(i,j) + p_atm(i,j) * IgR0
4347 enddo
4348 else
43490 IgR0 = 1.0 / (GV%Rho0 * GV%g_Earth)
43500 do i=is,ie
43510 ssh(i,j) = ssh(i,j) + p_atm(i,j) * IgR0
4352 enddo
4353 endif
4354 enddo
4355 !$omp target update to(ssh)
4356 endif
4357360end subroutine adjust_ssh_for_p_atm

4359!> Set the surface (return) properties of the ocean model by
4360!! setting the appropriate fields in sfc_state. Unused fields
4361!! are set to NULL or are unallocated.
4362361subroutine extract_surface_state(CS, sfc_state_in)

4366 !! data in this structure is intent out.

4378 real :: depth_ml !< Depth over which to average to determine mixed
4379 !! layer properties [Z ~> m] or [H ~> m or kg m-2]

4643361 if (allocated(sfc_state%melt_potential)) then
